data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/components/ExpensePieChart.tsx","content":"import { FC, useMemo } from 'react';\nimport { ResponsiveContainer, PieChart, Pie, Cell, Tooltip } from 'recharts';\nimport { ExpensePieChartProps, ExpenseItem } from '../types';\n\ninterface CustomTooltipProps {\n  active?: boolean;\n  payload?: Array<{\n    payload: ExpenseItem;\n  }>;\n}\n\ninterface PieClickData {\n  id?: string;\n  payload?: ExpenseItem;\n  [key: string]: unknown;\n}\n\nconst CustomTooltip: FC<CustomTooltipProps> = ({ active, payload }) => {\n  if (active && payload && payload.length > 0) {\n    const data = payload[0].payload;\n    return (\n      <div className=\"chart-tooltip\">\n        <p className=\"chart-tooltip-title\">{data.category}</p>\n        <p className=\"chart-tooltip-amount\">¥{data.amount.toLocaleString()}</p>\n        {data.note && (\n          <p className=\"chart-tooltip-note\">{data.note}</p>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n  return null;\n};\n\nconst ExpensePieChart: FC<ExpensePieChartProps> = ({\n  items = [],\n  selectedId,\n  onSelect,\n}) => {\n  const totalAmount = useMemo(() => {\n    return items.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.amount, 0);\n  }, [items]);\n\n  const selectedItem = useMemo(() => {\n    return items.find((item) => item.id === selectedId) || null;\n  }, [items, selectedId]);\n\n  const selectedPercentage = useMemo(() => {\n    if (!selectedItem || totalAmount === 0) return 0;\n    return Math.round((selectedItem.amount / totalAmount) * 1000) / 10;\n  }, [selectedItem, totalAmount]);\n\n  const handlePieClick = (entry?: PieClickData | ExpenseItem | null) => {\n    if (!entry) return;\n    const targetId =\n      'id' in entry && typeof entry.id === 'string'\n        ? entry.id\n        : entry.payload?.id;\n\n    if (!targetId) return;\n\n    if (selectedId === targetId) {\n      onSelect?.(null);\n    } else {\n      onSelect?.(targetId);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"chart-card\">\n      <div className=\"chart-wrapper\">\n        {items.length === 0 ? (\n          <div className=\"empty-message\">支出データがありません</div>\n        ) : (\n          <ResponsiveContainer width=\"100%\" height=\"100%\">\n            <PieChart>\n              <Pie\n                data={items}\n                dataKey=\"amount\"\n                nameKey=\"category\"\n                cx=\"50%\"\n                cy=\"50%\"\n                innerRadius={65}\n                outerRadius={105}\n                paddingAngle={3}\n                onClick={(entry) => handlePieClick(entry)}\n                cursor=\"pointer\"\n              >\n                {items.map((entry) => {\n                  const isSelected = entry.id === selectedId;\n                  return (\n                    <Cell\n                      key={`cell-${entry.id}`}\n                      fill={entry.color}\n                      stroke={isSelected ? '#1e293b' : '#ffffff'}\n                      strokeWidth={isSelected ? 3 : 1}\n                      style={{\n                        outline: 'none',\n                        transition: 'transform 0.2s ease',\n                        transform: isSelected ? 'scale(1.04)' : 'scale(1)',\n                        transformOrigin: 'center center',\n                      }}\n                    />\n                  );\n                })}\n              </Pie>\n              <Tooltip content={<CustomTooltip />} />\n            </PieChart>\n          </ResponsiveContainer>\n        )}\n      </div>\n\n      {items.length === 0 ? (\n        <div className=\"selected-info-box\">\n          <div className=\"selected-info-title\">データが登録されていません</div>\n          <div className=\"selected-info-note\">\n            下のフォームから支出を登録してください\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      ) : selectedItem ? (\n        <div className=\"selected-info-box\">\n          <div className=\"selected-info-title\">{selectedItem.category}</div>\n          <div className=\"selected-info-amount\">\n            ¥{selectedItem.amount.toLocaleString()}\n          </div>\n          <div>\n            <span className=\"selected-info-percent\">\n              {selectedPercentage}% (全体比)\n            </span>\n          </div>\n          {selectedItem.note && (\n            <div className=\"selected-info-note\">{selectedItem.note}</div>\n          )}\n        </div>\n      ) : (\n        <div className=\"selected-info-box\">\n          <div className=\"selected-info-title\">項目未選択</div>\n          <div className=\"selected-info-note\">\n            グラフや一覧の項目をクリックすると詳細が表示されます\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default ExpensePieChart;","language":"tsx"}

PRESET_CATEGORIES = [\n  { category: '食費', defaultColor: '#10b981' },\n  { category: '住居費', defaultColor: '#3b82f6' },\n  { category: '水道・光熱費', defaultColor: '#06b6d4' },\n  { category: '通信費', defaultColor: '#6366f1' },\n  { category: '日用品', defaultColor: '#8b5cf6' },\n  { category: '娯楽・交際費', defaultColor: '#f59e0b' },\n  { category: '保険・医療', defaultColor: '#ec4899' },\n  { category: '交通費', defaultColor: '#14b8a6' },\n  { category: '教育・教養', defaultColor: '#f97316' },\n  { category: 'その他', defaultColor: '#64748b' },\n];\n\nconst generateUniqueId = (): string => {\n  if (typeof crypto !== 'undefined' && typeof crypto.randomUUID === 'function') {\n    return `exp-${crypto.randomUUID()}`;\n  }\n  return `exp-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 9)}`;\n};\n\nconst App = () => {\n  const [items, setItems] = useState<ExpenseItem[]>(INITIAL_EXPENSES);\n  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<string | null>('exp-1');\n\n  // 新規登録フォーム用状態\n  const [category, setCategory] = useState<string>(PRESET_CATEGORIES[0].category);\n  const [amountInput, setAmountInput] = useState<string>('');\n  const [note, setNote] = useState<string>('');\n  const [errorMessage, setErrorMessage] = useState<string>('');\n\n  const totalAmount = useMemo(() => {\n    return items.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.amount, 0);\n  }, [items]);\n\n  const handleSelectItem = (id: string | null) => {\n    setSelectedId(id);\n  };\n\n  const handleDeleteItem = (id: string) => {\n    setItems((prev) => prev.filter((item) => item.id !== id));\n    if (selectedId === id) {\n      setSelectedId(null);\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleAddExpense = (e: FormEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault();\n    const parsedAmount = parseInt(amountInput, 10);\n    if (isNaN(parsedAmount) || parsedAmount <= 0) {\n      setErrorMessage('1円以上の有効な金額を入力してください。');\n      return;\n    }\n\n    setErrorMessage('');\n\n    const matchedPreset = PRESET_CATEGORIES.find((p) => p.category === category);\n    const color = matchedPreset ? matchedPreset.defaultColor : '#3b82f6';\n\n    const newItem: ExpenseItem = {\n      id: generateUniqueId(),\n      category,\n      amount: parsedAmount,\n      color,\n      note: note.trim(),\n    };\n\n    setItems((prev) => [newItem, ...prev]);\n    setSelectedId(newItem.id);\n    setAmountInput('');\n    setNote('');\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"app-container\">\n      <header className=\"app-header\">\n        <h1 className=\"app-title\">家計簿支出ダッシュボード</h1>\n        <p className=\"app-subtitle\">\n          支出の内訳を円グラフで可視化。グラフやリストをクリックして詳細を確認できます。\n        </p>\n      </header>\n\n      <section className=\"total-banner\">\n        <span className=\"total-label\">今月の支出合計</span>\n        <span className=\"total-value\">¥{totalAmount.toLocaleString()}</span>\n      </section>\n\n      <main className=\"dashboard-grid\">\n        <ExpensePieChart\n          items={items}\n          selectedId={selectedId}\n          onSelect={handleSelectItem}\n        />\n        <ExpenseList\n          items={items}\n          selectedId={selectedId}\n          onSelect={(id) => handleSelectItem(id === selectedId ? null : id)}\n          onDelete={handleDeleteItem}\n          totalAmount={totalAmount}\n        />\n      </main>\n\n      <section className=\"form-card\">\n        <h2 className=\"form-title\">支出を追加</h2>\n        <form className=\"expense-form\" onSubmit={handleAddExpense}>\n          <div className=\"form-row\">\n            <div className=\"form-group\">\n              <label className=\"form-label\" htmlFor=\"expense-category\">\n                カテゴリー\n              </label>\n              <select\n                id=\"expense-category\"\n                className=\"form-select\"\n                value={category}\n                onChange={(e) => setCategory(e.target.value)}\n              >\n                {PRESET_CATEGORIES.map((preset) => (\n                  <option key={preset.category} value={preset.category}>\n                    {preset.category}\n                  </option>\n                ))}\n              </select>\n            </div>\n\n            <div className=\"form-group\">\n              <label className=\"form-label\" htmlFor=\"expense-amount\">\n                金額 (円)\n              </label>\n              <input\n                id=\"expense-amount\"\n                type=\"number\"\n                min=\"1\"\n                step=\"1\"\n                placeholder=\"例: 5000\"\n                className=\"form-input\"\n                value={amountInput}\n                onChange={(e) => {\n                  setAmountInput(e.target.value);\n                  if (errorMessage) {\n                    setErrorMessage('');\n                  }\n                }}\n                required\n              />\n            </div>\n\n            <div className=\"form-group\">\n              <label className=\"form-label\" htmlFor=\"expense-note\">\n                メモ\n              </label>\n              <input\n                id=\"expense-note\"\n                type=\"text\"\n                placeholder=\"例: スーパーでの買い物\"\n                className=\"form-input\"\n                value={note}\n                onChange={(e) => setNote(e.target.value)}\n              />\n            </div>\n          </div>\n\n          {errorMessage && (\n            <p role=\"alert\" style={{ color: 'var(--color-danger)', fontSize: '0.85rem' }}>\n              {errorMessage}\n            </p>\n          )}\n\n          <button type=\"submit\" className=\"btn-add\">\n            <span>+</span> 支出を登録する\n          </button>\n        </form>\n      </section>\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default App;","language":"tsx"}

data: {"agent":"specialist-kaito","file":"src/components/ExpensePieChart.tsx","content":"import { FC, useMemo } from 'react';\nimport { ResponsiveContainer, PieChart, Pie, Cell, Tooltip } from 'recharts';\nimport { ExpensePieChartProps, ExpenseItem } from '../types';\n\ninterface CustomTooltipProps {\n  active?: boolean;\n  payload?: Array<{\n    payload: ExpenseItem;\n  }>;\n}\n\ninterface PieClickData {\n  id?: string;\n  payload?: ExpenseItem;\n  [key: string]: unknown;\n}\n\nconst CustomTooltip: FC<CustomTooltipProps> = ({ active, payload }) => {\n  if (active && payload && payload.length > 0) {\n    const data = payload[0].payload;\n    return (\n      <div className=\"chart-tooltip\">\n        <p className=\"chart-tooltip-title\">{data.category}</p>\n        <p className=\"chart-tooltip-amount\">¥{data.amount.toLocaleString()}</p>\n        {data.note && (\n          <p className=\"chart-tooltip-note\">{data.note}</p>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n  return null;\n};\n\nconst ExpensePieChart: FC<ExpensePieChartProps> = ({\n  items = [],\n  selectedId,\n  onSelect,\n}) => {\n  const totalAmount = useMemo(() => {\n    return items.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.amount, 0);\n  }, [items]);\n\n  const selectedItem = useMemo(() => {\n    return items.find((item) => item.id === selectedId) || null;\n  }, [items, selectedId]);\n\n  const selectedPercentage = useMemo(() => {\n    if (!selectedItem || totalAmount === 0) return 0;\n    return Math.round((selectedItem.amount / totalAmount) * 1000) / 10;\n  }, [selectedItem, totalAmount]);\n\n  const handlePieClick = (entry?: PieClickData | ExpenseItem | null) => {\n    if (!entry) return;\n    const targetId =\n      'id' in entry && typeof entry.id === 'string'\n        ? entry.id\n        : entry.payload?.id;\n\n    if (!targetId) return;\n\n    if (selectedId === targetId) {\n      onSelect?.(null);\n    } else {\n      onSelect?.(targetId);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"chart-card\">\n      <div className=\"chart-wrapper\">\n        {items.length === 0 ? (\n          <div className=\"empty-message\">支出データがありません</div>\n        ) : (\n          <ResponsiveContainer width=\"100%\" height=\"100%\">\n            <PieChart>\n              <Pie\n                data={items}\n                dataKey=\"amount\"\n                nameKey=\"category\"\n                cx=\"50%\"\n                cy=\"50%\"\n                innerRadius={65}\n                outerRadius={105}\n                paddingAngle={3}\n                onClick={(entry) => handlePieClick(entry)}\n                cursor=\"pointer\"\n              >\n                {items.map((entry) => {\n                  const isSelected = entry.id === selectedId;\n                  return (\n                    <Cell\n                      key={`cell-${entry.id}`}\n                      fill={entry.color}\n                      stroke={isSelected ? '#1e293b' : '#ffffff'}\n                      strokeWidth={isSelected ? 3 : 1}\n                      style={{\n                        outline: 'none',\n                        transition: 'transform 0.2s ease',\n                        transform: isSelected ? 'scale(1.04)' : 'scale(1)',\n                        transformOrigin: 'center center',\n                      }}\n                    />\n                  );\n                })}\n              </Pie>\n              <Tooltip content={<CustomTooltip />} />\n            </PieChart>\n          </ResponsiveContainer>\n        )}\n      </div>\n\n      {items.length === 0 ? (\n        <div className=\"selected-info-box\">\n          <div className=\"selected-info-title\">データが登録されていません</div>\n          <div className=\"selected-info-note\">\n            下のフォームから支出を登録してください\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      ) : selectedItem ? (\n        <div className=\"selected-info-box\">\n          <div className=\"selected-info-title\">{selectedItem.category}</div>\n          <div className=\"selected-info-amount\">\n            ¥{selectedItem.amount.toLocaleString()}\n          </div>\n          <div>\n            <span className=\"selected-info-percent\">\n              {selectedPercentage}% (全体比)\n            </span>\n          </div>\n          {selectedItem.note && (\n            <div className=\"selected-info-note\">{selectedItem.note}</div>\n          )}\n        </div>\n      ) : (\n        <div className=\"selected-info-box\">\n          <div className=\"selected-info-title\">項目未選択</div>\n          <div className=\"selected-info-note\">\n            グラフや一覧の項目をクリックすると詳細が表示されます\n          </div>\n        </div>\n      )}\n    </div>\n  );\n};\n\nexport default ExpensePieChart;","language":"tsx"}

Tooltip } from 'recharts';\nimport { ExpensePieChartProps, ExpenseItem } from '../types';\n\ninterface CustomTooltipProps {\n  active?: boolean;\n  payload?: Array<{\n    payload: ExpenseItem;\n  }>;\n}\n\ninterface PieClickData {\n  id?: string;\n  payload?: ExpenseItem;\n  [key: string]: unknown;\n}\n\nconst CustomTooltip: FC<CustomTooltipProps> = ({ active, payload }) => {\n  if (active && payload && payload.length > 0) {\n    const data = payload[0].payload;\n    return (\n      <div className=\"chart-tooltip\">\n        <p className=\"chart-tooltip-title\">{data.category}</p>\n        <p className=\"chart-tooltip-amount\">¥{data.amount.toLocaleString()}</p>\n        {data.note && (\n          <p className=\"chart-tooltip-note\">{data.note}</p>\n        )}\n      </div>\n    );\n  }\n  return null;\n};\n\nconst ExpensePieChart: FC<ExpensePieChartProps> = ({\n  items = [],\n  selectedId,\n  onSelect,\n}) => {\n  const totalAmount = useMemo(() => {\n    return items.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.amount, 0);\n  }, [items]);\n\n  const selectedItem = useMemo(() => {\n    return items.find((item) => item.id === selectedId) || null;\n  }, [items, selectedId]);\n\n  const selectedPercentage = useMemo(() => {\n    if (!selectedItem || totalAmount === 0) return 0;\n    return Math.round((selectedItem.amount / totalAmount) * 1000) / 10;\n  }, [selectedItem, totalAmount]);\n\n  const handlePieClick = (entry?: PieClickData | ExpenseItem | null) => {\n    if (!entry) return;\n    const targetId =\n      'id' in entry && typeof entry.id === 'string'\n        ? entry.id\n        : entry.payload?.id;\n\n    if (!targetId) return;\n\n    if (selectedId === targetId) {\n      onSelect?.(null);\n    } else {\n      onSelect?.(targetId);\n    }\n  };\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"chart-card\">\n      <div className=\"chart-wrapper\">\n        {items.length === 0 ? (\n          <div className=\"empty-message\">支出データがありません</div>\n        ) : (\n          <ResponsiveContainer width=\"100%\" height=\"100%\">\n            <PieChart>\n              <Pie\n                data={items}\n                dataKey=\"amount\"\n                nameKey=\"category\"\n                cx=\"50%\"\n                cy=\"50%\"\n                innerRadius={65}\n                outerRadius={105}\n                paddingAngle={3}\n                onClick={(entry) => handlePieClick(entry)}\n                cursor=\"pointer\"\n              >\n                {items.map((entry) => {\n                  const isSelected = entry.id === selectedId;\n                  return (\n                    <Cell\n                      key={`cell-${entry.id}`}\n                      fill={entry.color}\n                      stroke={isSelected ?
